## **THE GLOOM MISSION - THORNMAR WORLD**

### **Mission Context:**

**Guild:** Karathos Guild  
**Contract Type:** Dungeon Clearance (Loot Rights)  
**World:** Thornmar (Tier 2, Medieval-Fantasy)  
**Target:** The Gloom - Tier 2 Legendary corrupted dungeon  
**Hazard Level:** High (corrupted mana, environmental poison, elite enemies)

### **Pre-Mission Preparation:**

**Loadout:**
- Compound bow (primary ranged)
- Twin tactical tomahawks (melee)
- Glock 19 (backup, suppressed)
- HK416 rifle (stored, emergency)
- Full tactical gear + respirator (corruption protection)
- Dogs: Eisenhower, Zhukov, Rommel (full combat deployment)

**Entry Time:** 4:00 PM Saturday (Earth time)  
**Thornmar Local Time:** 1:13 PM

### **Mission Execution:**

**Phase 1 - Outer Chambers (1:13 PM - 1:47 PM Thornmar time):**
- Cleared 8 Gloom Wraiths (Tier 2 Uncommon, Level 28-31)
- Cleared 4 Corrupted Sentinels (Tier 2 Rare, Level 33-35)
- Environmental hazard: Corrupted mana pools (avoided via PER checks)
- Dogs performed excellently - coordinated pack tactics
- Zero injuries sustained

**Phase 2 - Deep Gloom (1:47 PM - 2:23 PM):**
- Encountered 2 Gloom Stalkers (Tier 2 Epic, Level 38-39) - Elite ambush predators
- First Stalker: Eisenhower distraction → Ryan tomahawk crit (skull strike) → 1-shot kill
- Second Stalker: Coordinated dog attack (Zhukov hamstring) → Ryan finishing blow
- Boss Chamber: Corrupted Gloom Core (environmental boss, no combat)
  - Purification required: Willpower check (Ryan: 94 vs DC 85) - SUCCESS
  - Core destroyed via Resolve Aura channeling (10 minute ritual)

**Phase 3 - Loot & Exit (2:23 PM - 2:41 PM):**
- Dungeon collapsed after core destruction (standard cleared-dungeon mechanics)
- Loot collected from boss chamber and elite kills
- Returned to Thornmar surface: 2:41 PM local (6:47 PM Earth time)

**Mission Duration:**
- Thornmar Time: 1 hour 28 minutes
- Earth Time: 2 hours 47 minutes (time dilation: 1.9× faster on Thornmar)

## **GLOOM MISSION - XP GAINS**

### **Enemy XP Breakdown:**

**8× Gloom Wraiths (Tier 2 Uncommon, Lv28-31):**
- Base: 30 XP each × 2 (Tier 2) × 1 (Standard role) = 60 XP each
- Total: 480 XP

**4× Corrupted Sentinels (Tier 2 Rare, Lv33-35):**
- Base: 34 XP each × 4 (Tier 2 Rare) × 1 (Standard role) = 136 XP each
- Total: 544 XP

**2× Gloom Stalkers (Tier 2 Epic, Lv38-39):**
- Base: 38.5 XP average × 8 (Tier 2 Epic) × 2 (Elite role) = 616 XP each

- Tier 2 Legendary World-Hazard

- Clean Execution: ×1.2 (zero casualties, full objective)

**Mission XP:** 800 × 1.5 × 1.3 × 1.2 = **1,872 XP**

### **BALLISTIC CALCULATIONS - ADJUSTMENTS NEEDED**

**Elevation Impact (+4,883 feet):**
- Thinner air = less drag
- Bullets fly **flatter** and **faster**
- Impact: ~0.5-0.8 MOA **less** elevation needed per distance

**Temperature Impact (-37°F):**
- Colder air = denser air = **more** drag
- Powder burns slightly slower = reduced velocity
- Impact: ~0.3-0.5 MOA **more** elevation needed per distance

**Barometric Pressure Impact (-6.4 inHg):**
- Lower pressure = less drag (altitude effect)
- Impact: Already factored into elevation calculation above

**Net Effect:** Elevation change dominates, temperature partially offsets.

**KAYLEE - SITTING IN G-WAGON:**

Not bad, just **noticeably different** from the GLE.

## **DAVID'S ANSWER - PRICE DIFFERENCE EXPLANATION**

**DAVID:**

Walks over to stand next to you while Kaylee explores the G-Wagon.

---

**"Great question. The price difference comes down to three things:**

---

### **1. BRAND POSITIONING & DEMAND**

**"The G-Wagon is Mercedes' **halo vehicle**—their icon."**

Gestures to the boxy shape.

**"It's been in continuous production since 1979. Originally a military vehicle, now it's a status symbol."**

**"People pay a premium because:**
- It's **recognizable** (everyone knows what a G-Wagon is)
- It's a **celebrity car** (Kardashians, athletes, rappers—G-Wagon is THE luxury SUV)
- **Limited production** (Mercedes intentionally keeps supply lower than demand)
- **Holds value insanely well** (3-year-old G-Wagons sell for 80-85% of original price)

**"You're paying $50k extra for the **badge and the status**, not necessarily better engineering."**

---

### **2. MANUFACTURING COST**

**"The G-Wagon is expensive to build:**

**Body-on-frame construction:**
- Separate ladder frame + body panels (like your Delica or a truck)
- More expensive to manufacture than unibody (like the GLE)
- Built by hand in **Graz, Austria** (specialized factory, low volume)

**Three locking differentials:**
- Front, center, rear locking diffs are **expensive** mechanical systems
- Most buyers never use them, but they're there

**Portal axles** (on higher trims):
- Raises the vehicle without lifting (better ground clearance without lift kit)
- Complicated, expensive engineering

**"The GLE is mass-produced on a shared platform (same basic chassis as E-Class sedan). Economy of scale makes it cheaper."**

---

### **3. TARGET MARKET**

**"Here's the honest truth:**

**GLE buyers** want luxury, performance, comfort—**practical luxury SUV**

**G-Wagon buyers** want to be **seen** in a G-Wagon

**"The G-Wagon is a **fashion statement**. It's the Rolex of SUVs—you're paying for heritage, recognition, and exclusivity."**

**"The GLE is the **Omega**—technically superior in many ways, but doesn't have the same cultural cachet."**

---

**DAVID CONTINUES:**

**"Don't get me wrong—the G-Wagon is **capable** and **well-built**. But if you're comparing:**

**GLE 53 AMG:**
- $90k-$100k
- Faster (5.2s 0-60 vs 5.6s)
- More comfortable
- Better tech
- Better daily driver

**G 550:**
- $140k-$150k
- Slower
- Stiffer ride
- Older tech
- But **everyone notices it**

**"You're paying $50k extra for people to look at you when you pull up."**

Beat.

**"Is that worth it to you?"**

---

## **KAYLEE - FROM INSIDE G-WAGON**

She's sitting in the driver's seat.

Adjusting.

- **Steering wheel:** Feels heavier, less refined

"No one knows about my plan with Dan or building an army, as you phrased it." I fix her beanie and smile "Just you."

I interlace my fingers from both hands into hers, pulling her onto my lap to straddle me. "You're my girlfriend. I would literally lose my mind if something happened to you, Kay." I wait "But, the part of this multiverse thing I haven't been clear with you on, is that in like, 450 days, everyone is going to have to be part of it. Either earth wins this little competition of induction and we survive, or we get 2nd or 3rd place and we become a Dungeon World, about 80% of the population dies and earth turns into a series of monster dungeon spots other aliens from the multiverse pay to fight through for experience and loot while we try to hold on. Or we get 4th or 5th place and the planet is scrapped for resources, earth goes away." I squeeze her hands to stabilize her. "Right now I have no idea where the other planets being inducted are or where they are in the points race, this just started like, a week ago."

I take a breather, watching her. "It's going to be okay Kay. I'm going to win. I always win." I hold her eye contact. I hold her for a few second. "But I can't risk you getting hurt. It would break me and all of earth would suffer. I nod to her sitting on my lap in a cute outfit in a warm car with me. "I need you in my corner, a safe, warm, loving place I can shelter from the universal storm when I drag my bruised and bloody body back through the portal to earth between missions." I kiss her forehead. "PLease Kaylee Hayes. Please"

"I said it before, I'll say it again. We need to stop putting the NBA on a pedastol. They players are unbelievably talented. The spectacle is off the charts, but if we hyperfocus on that, we lose why we love the game. The comradery, the trash talk, the athleticism, the community, the culture. Call me an old head, but I want people to find joy in the steak, not joy in the sizzle."
